serialize graph mutations to prevent concurrent write race (#4555)\n\ncreateEntities, createRelations, addObservations, deleteEntities,\ndeleteObservations, and deleteRelations each independently did\nload -> mutate -> save with no synchronization. Concurrent tool\ncalls (e.g. multiple mutations dispatched from one LLM turn) could\nrace: both read the same starting state, both write back their own\ncopy, and whichever write landed last silently discarded the other's\nchanges. create_directory doesn't create parent directories (#4631)\n\nvalidatePath()'s ENOENT fallback checked only the immediate parent\ndirectory before allowing a new path through. Creating a path with\nmore than one missing level (e.g. a/b/c when none of a, b, c exist)\nmade the immediate parent check fail with \"Parent directory does not\nexist\", even though fs.mkdir was already called with recursive: true\none level up in the create_directory handler and an existing allowed\nancestor was available further up the tree.\n\nWalk up through missing ancestors until an existing one is found (or\nthe filesystem root is reached), verifying that ancestor still\nresolves inside an allowed directory via realpath. The top-level\nlexical containment check already ran against the full target path,\nso this only extends how far the existence/symlink check climbs.\n\nFixes #4629\n\nCo-authored-by: Riley <313779891+rileybuilds@users.noreply.github.com>\nCo-authored-by: olaservo <olahungerford@gmail.com>\nCo-authored-by: Claude Fable 5 <noreply@anthropic.com>","tree":{"sha":"700411be4ecc7528b8b4f4635b77c2a0bb259610","url":"https://api.github.com/repos/modelcontextprotocol/servers/git/trees/700411be4ecc7528b8b4f4635b77c2a0bb259610"},"url":"https://api.github.com/repos/modelcontextprotocol/servers/git/commits/cda92bdaacd558192fedf1a60d2bb27510792388","comment_count":0,"verification":{"verified":true,"reason":"valid","signature":"-----BEGIN PGP SIGNATURE-----\n\nwsFcBAABCAAQBQJqkcw7CRC1aQ7uu5UhlAAAiGUQAEb4hxL74ARuOlZ9dgJSMzQB\nm3yg5cE+YJ83kK9VfvHoa56LlwTjeZPJFcZ4D2M+KSkItigEq7YQh/O48VVZDXFJ\nCkcnHS6LTHyMvf4XDT8qlBKp6rxmhqyjV8k2APbu6EAWgRETT6xJD+9MRoYq+3rf\ndIoGjIDaiAgU/r1ci0rsCC/ifHf7tFY7cVkBE9H3fGNZIj1+duSk+cPfaf7XwnK7\nYCgHadTVmX5zxpgIlM/Cl/8HNTyqcJ8HM4yk2M/7dHXtWnMEjGhsgLp8PI3U94KS\ndYrIPwBmz87tfvzubFwA7hWLpf+rh0gbpIoFxqgmbSQ52YMcn21l4EFdg+N9lDVM\nutJNNjLziM9jXFHKz6UrX9umeLhmrOICoZgkChwPR6ip1PfyH1uiwNYHN66wiUb0\nC+FTLJmFQKL+skEjzKDJC14g8sey/7vWJk1T6m69Rc7Lj3Ji6GZIRU8P9XhLjj6Q\na3cjY7lY+wtpW6b7R58igQvZp8yHmlboYkXG5hb3hgEyYR9WYpTw3YJF1/313qWg\nktqjHaCK3ha8tVsF6DFXIqDohQvEhb2i9w4gveT/gq83s2cwf5kmMNgt85H0ylfR\n4vykIFCnoQsiCdRJX/63lDD4dzfWmHAq8ckm1olIgza0g/tx0V5bvLGS4qw79XPc\nYE8rp5uw6MNjFFfu4Y8r\n=UlFC\n-----END PGP SIGNATURE-----\n","payload":"tree 700411be4ecc7528b8b4f4635b77c2a0bb259610\nparent d6402efee6cc6446b23adbcb63051f9dc8612be5\nauthor Riley <kirkhopegrant@gmail.com> 1787939899 +0100\ncommitter GitHub <noreply@github.com> 1787939899 -0700\n\nfix(filesystem): create_directory doesn't create parent directories (#4631)\n\nvalidatePath()'s ENOENT fallback checked only the immediate parent\ndirectory before allowing a new path through. write the knowledge graph atomically (#4642)\n\n* fix(memory): write the knowledge graph atomically\n\n`saveGraph()` wrote the graph straight to the memory file:\n\n    await fs.writeFile(this.memoryFilePath, lines.join(\"\\n\"));\n\n`fs.writeFile` opens the target with `'w'`, which truncates it before any\nnew bytes are written. If the process is interrupted between truncation\nand completion — SIGKILL, container or host stop, OOM kill, power loss —\nthe memory file is left empty or half-written.\n\nThat file is the only persistence layer for the knowledge graph, so the\nwindow is small but the loss is total and unrecoverable.\n\nThe fix writes to a temporary file in the same directory and renames it\nover the target. `rename(2)` is atomic on POSIX filesystems: a reader\nsees either the complete old file or the complete new one, never an\nintermediate state. Keeping the temp file in the same directory ensures\nthe rename stays on one filesystem, since cross-device renames fail with\nEXDEV. On failure the temp file is removed so no strays accumulate.\n\nTests added in `__tests__/atomic-save.test.ts`:\n\n- the live memory file is never opened for truncating writes\n- a committed graph survives a write that fails midway\n- no temporary files remain after a successful write\n- the temporary file is cleaned up when the rename fails\n- graph contents still round-trip correctly across reloads\n\nThe first and fourth fail against the previous implementation.\n\nFull suite: 55 passed (50 existing, 5 new). `tsc --noEmit` clean.\n\nFixes #4614\n\n* fix(memory): use randomBytes for temp filename to avoid concurrent collision\n\n---------\n\nCo-authored-by: teddiesloco make move_file fail instead of overwriting the destination (#4630)\n\nThe move_file tool description and README both state the operation fails if\nthe destination already exists, but the handler called fs.rename directly,\nwhich silently overwrites the destination. Those prefixes are mutually\n  exclusive, so the condition is always false and the branch is dead.\n  Drop it; the SDK's template-based routing already guarantees the URI\n  prefix is one of the two before the handler runs.
